# fix: free CudaGraphManager runners in delete_cuda_graphs after capture

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8557) · state: closed · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Summary
`delete_cuda_graphs()` only reset runners still listed in `cudagraph_record` / `cudagraph_inference_record`. `create_cudagraphs()` clears that record after capture, so training runners that live on each `CudaGraphManager` were left holding graph/mempool references (hangs on process teardown; see Megatron-Bridge WAR).

Track live `CudaGraphManager` instances and free their runners (and clear the lookup table) during delete.

Closes #8537

## Test plan
- [x] Unit test: post-capture empty record still nulls manager runners and clears manager lists
